Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t base = r * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points.  This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering).  Raw aligned allocations are used\n    // instead of std::vector so that the buffers are not pre-faulted by a\n    // single-threaded zero fill; the parallel first touch below distributes the\n    // pages across the NUMA nodes according to the loop partitioning.\n    constexpr size_t kAlign = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(Real) + kAlign - 1) / kAlign) * kAlign;\n    Real* grid1 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    Real* grid2 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    if (grid1 == nullptr || grid2 == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grids\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    initializeGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);  // first touch of the second buffer\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalGridVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalGridVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4127,"cache_read_input_tokens":20864,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4127},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fee40687-2a17-45c0-a7b5-2ad3ff195716","uuid":"69a1ec01-ab01-464f-bc63-a6a71e58e370","timestamp":"2026-09-06T23:36:43.569Z","request_id":"req_011Ceo4dY2yZD5gzv47Jdhng"}

This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering).  Raw aligned allocations are used\n    // instead of std::vector so that the buffers are not pre-faulted by a\n    // single-threaded zero fill; the parallel first touch below distributes the\n    // pages across the NUMA nodes according to the loop partitioning.\n    constexpr size_t kAlign = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(Real) + kAlign - 1) / kAlign) * kAlign;\n    Real* grid1 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    Real* grid2 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    if (grid1 == nullptr || grid2 == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grids\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    initializeGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);  // first touch of the second buffer\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalGridVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalGridVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":6,"oldLines":6,"newStart":6,"newLines":8,"lines":[" #include <cstring>"," #include <vector>"," ","+#include <omp.h>","+"," #include \"../common/results_output.hpp\""," "," using Real = double;"]},{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":84,"newStart":17,"newLines":105,"lines":["     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;"," }"," ","-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {","-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {","-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {","-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {","-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);","-                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;","-            }","+// The grid is partitioned over threads by rows of the x-fastest layout, i.e. by","+// the flattened index r = z * ny + y. If the user did not configure OpenMP affinity\n// explicitly we pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core first,\n// SMT siblings only once every core is in use.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; If the user did not configure OpenMP affinity\n// explicitly we pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core first,\n// SMT siblings only once every core is in use.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t base = r * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points.  This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. If the user did not configure OpenMP affinity\n// explicitly we pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core first,\n// SMT siblings only once every core is in use.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n            if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n        }\n    }\n    std::fclose(f);\n}\n\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;  // respect an explicit user configuration\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    std::vector<int> order;                       // physical cores first, then SMT siblings\n    std::vector<int> smt;\n    std::vector<char> claimed(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        order.push_back(c);\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            smt.push_back(s);\n        }\n    }\n    order.insert(order.end(), smt.begin(), smt.end());\n    if (order.empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t numCpus = order.size();\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(order[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % numCpus], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// The grid is partitioned over threads by rows of the x-fastest layout, i.e. by\n// the flattened index r = z * ny + y.  Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t base = r * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points.  This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    bindThreadsIfUnconfigured();\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering).  Raw aligned allocations are used\n    // instead of std::vector so that the buffers are not pre-faulted by a\n    // single-threaded zero fill; the parallel first touch below distributes the\n    // pages across the NUMA nodes according to the loop partitioning.\n    constexpr size_t kAlign = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(Real) + kAlign - 1) / kAlign) * kAlign;\n    Real* grid1 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    Real* grid2 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    if (grid1 == nullptr || grid2 == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grids\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    initializeGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);  // first touch of the second buffer\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalGridVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalGridVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":54,"oldLines":26,"newStart":54,"newLines":42,"lines":["     CPU_ZERO(&allowed);","     if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;"," ","-    std::vector<int> order; This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalGridVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalGridVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":44,"oldLines":12,"newStart":44,"newLines":7,"lines":["     std::fclose(f);"," }"," ","-static void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {","-    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||","-        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {","-        return;  // respect an explicit user configuration","-    }","-","+static void configureThreads() {","     cpu_set_t allowed;","     CPU_ZERO(&allowed);","     if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; Every loop over the grid (initialization\n115\t// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n116\t// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n117\t// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n118\t// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n119\t\n120\tvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n121\t    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n122\t#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n123\t    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n124\t        const size_t base = r * nx;\n125\t        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n126\t            const size_t idx = base + x;\n127\t            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n128\t        }\n129\t    }\n130\t}\n131\t\n132\t// 7-point stencil computation\n133\tvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n134\t                      Real* __restrict output,\n135\t                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n136\t    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n137\t    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n138\t\n139\t    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n140\t    // cell and is simply copied over.\n141\t    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n142\t#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n143\t        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n144\t            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n145\t        }\n146\t        return;\n147\t    }\n148\t\n149\t    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n150\t    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points. Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        std::vector<int> core{c};\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            core.push_back(s);\n        }\n        cores.push_back(std::move(core));\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    // SMT siblings buy very little for a bandwidth bound kernel and cost cache\n    // capacity, so default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n    }\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them. Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n            if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n        }\n    }\n    std::fclose(f);\n}\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs by physical core.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cores;\n    std::vector<char> claimed(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        std::vector<int> core{c};\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            core.push_back(s);\n        }\n        cores.push_back(std::move(core));\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    // SMT siblings buy very little for a bandwidth bound kernel and cost cache\n    // capacity, so default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n    }\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t base = r * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.  Must be called from inside a parallel region;\n// the worksharing constructs carry the implicit barrier that separates two\n// consecutive sweeps, so the iteration loop never has to fork a team.\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n            if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n        }\n    }\n    std::fclose(f);\n}\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs by physical core.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cores;\n    std::vector<char> claimed(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        std::vector<int> core{c};\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            core.push_back(s);\n        }\n        cores.push_back(std::move(core));\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    // SMT siblings buy very little for a bandwidth bound kernel and cost cache\n    // capacity, so default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n    }\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;  // an explicit placement policy was requested; leave it alone\n    }\n\n    // Candidate CPUs, one entry per physical core; SMT siblings are appended\n    // core-by-core so that an oversubscribed team places *neighbouring* threads\n    // (which work on neighbouring data) on the same core.\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    for (const auto& core : cores) order.push_back(core[0]);\n    if (numThreads > cores.size()) {\n        order.clear();\n        for (const auto& core : cores) order.insert(order.end(), core.begin(), core.end());\n    }\n    const size_t numCpus = order.size();\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        // Spread a small team evenly over the machine instead of crowding the\n        // first socket; wrap around once every CPU carries a thread.\n        const size_t slot = (numThreads < numCpus) ? (tid * numCpus) / numThreads : tid % numCpus;\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(order[slot], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// The grid is partitioned over threads by rows of the x-fastest layout, i.e. by\n// the flattened index r = z * ny + y.  Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t base = r * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.  Must be called from inside a parallel region;\n// the worksharing constructs carry the implicit barrier that separates two\n// consecutive sweeps, so the iteration loop never has to fork a team.\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points.  This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        out[0] = in[0];\n        out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n\n        // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so results\n        // are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering).  Raw aligned allocations are used\n    // instead of std::vector so that the buffers are not pre-faulted by a\n    // single-threaded zero fill; the parallel first touch below distributes the\n    // pages across the NUMA nodes according to the loop partitioning.\n    constexpr size_t kAlign = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(Real) + kAlign - 1) / kAlign) * kAlign;\n    Real* grid1 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    Real* grid2 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    if (grid1 == nullptr || grid2 == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grids\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    initializeGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);  // first touch of the second buffer\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n            } else {\n                stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n            if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n        }\n    }\n    std::fclose(f);\n}\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs by physical core.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cores;\n    std::vector<char> claimed(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        std::vector<int> core{c};\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            core.push_back(s);\n        }\n        cores.push_back(std::move(core));\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    // SMT siblings buy very little for a bandwidth bound kernel and cost cache\n    // capacity, so default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n    }\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; wrap around once every CPU carries a thread.\n        const size_t slot = (numThreads < numCpus) ? (tid * numCpus) / numThreads : tid % numCpus;\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(order[slot], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// The grid is partitioned over threads by rows of the x-fastest layout, i.e. by\n// the flattened index r = z * ny + y.  Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\n// Contiguous, balanced block of rows owned by the calling thread.  Used by every\n// grid traversal so that ownership -- and therefore page placement -- is stable.\nstatic inline void rowRange(const size_t numRows, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n    const size_t quot = numRows / numThreads;\n    const size_t rem = numRows % numThreads;\n    begin = tid * quot + std::min(tid, rem);\n    end = begin + quot + (tid < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t rBegin, rEnd;\n        rowRange(numRows, rBegin, rEnd);\n        for (size_t idx = rBegin * nx; idx < rEnd * nx; ++idx) {\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.  Must be called from inside a parallel region;\n// the worksharing constructs carry the implicit barrier that separates two\n// consecutive sweeps, so the iteration loop never has to fork a team.\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(output + r * nx, input + r * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points. Unless the user configured OpenMP explicitly we\n// therefore choose the team size (one thread per physical core) and pin the\n// threads ourselves, spread evenly over all cores/sockets.\n\n// Parses a sysfs CPU list such as \"0,128\" or \"0-3,8\" into `out`.\nstatic void parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& out) {\n    out.clear();\n    FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return;\n    char buf[512];\n    if (std::fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr) {\n        const char* p = buf;\n        while (*p != '\\0') {\n            char* endp = nullptr;\n            const long first = std::strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n            if (endp == p) break;\n            long last = first;\n            p = endp;\n            if (*p == '-') {\n                last = std::strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n                p = endp;\n            }\n            for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n            if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n        }\n    }\n    std::fclose(f);\n}\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs by physical core.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cores;\n    std::vector<char> claimed(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed) || claimed[c]) continue;\n        claimed[c] = 1;\n        std::vector<int> core{c};\n        char path[128];\n        std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        parseCpuList(path, siblings);\n        for (const int s : siblings) {\n            if (s < 0 || s >= CPU_SETSIZE || claimed[s] || !CPU_ISSET(s, &allowed)) continue;\n            claimed[s] = 1;\n            core.push_back(s);\n        }\n        cores.push_back(std::move(core));\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    // SMT siblings buy very little for a bandwidth bound kernel and cost cache\n    // capacity, so default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cores.size()));\n    }\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;  // an explicit placement policy was requested; leave it alone\n    }\n\n    // Candidate CPUs, one entry per physical core; SMT siblings are appended\n    // core-by-core so that an oversubscribed team places *neighbouring* threads\n    // (which work on neighbouring data) on the same core.\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    for (const auto& core : cores) order.push_back(core[0]);\n    if (numThreads > cores.size()) {\n        order.clear();\n        for (const auto& core : cores) order.insert(order.end(), core.begin(), core.end());\n    }\n    const size_t numCpus = order.size();\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        // Spread a small team evenly over the machine instead of crowding the\n        // first socket; wrap around once every CPU carries a thread.\n        const size_t slot = (numThreads < numCpus) ? (tid * numCpus) / numThreads : tid % numCpus;\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(order[slot], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// The grid is partitioned over threads by rows of the x-fastest layout, i.e. by\n// the flattened index r = z * ny + y.  Every loop over the grid (initialization\n// included) uses the very same static partitioning, so that the pages of each\n// row are first-touched by the thread that will keep operating on them.  On a\n// multi-socket machine this keeps the (bandwidth bound) stencil traffic local to\n// the NUMA node of the owning thread.\n\n// Contiguous, balanced block of rows owned by the calling thread.  Used by every\n// grid traversal so that ownership -- and therefore page placement -- is stable.\nstatic inline void rowRange(const size_t numRows, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    const size_t numThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n    const size_t quot = numRows / numThreads;\n    const size_t rem = numRows % numThreads;\n    begin = tid * quot + std::min(tid, rem);\n    end = begin + quot + (tid < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t rBegin, rEnd;\n        rowRange(numRows, rBegin, rEnd);\n        for (size_t idx = rBegin * nx; idx < rEnd * nx; ++idx) {\n            grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.  Must be called from inside a parallel region;\n// the worksharing constructs carry the implicit barrier that separates two\n// consecutive sweeps, so the iteration loop never has to fork a team.\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t numRows = ny * nz;\n\n    size_t rBegin, rEnd;\n    rowRange(numRows, rBegin, rEnd);\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior points at all: every cell is a boundary\n    // cell and is simply copied over.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n        std::memcpy(output + rBegin * nx, input + rBegin * nx, (rEnd - rBegin) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n#pragma omp barrier\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // A whole row (fixed y, z) is either entirely boundary -- and then just\n    // copied -- or interior except for its two end points.  This replaces the\n    // original full-grid boundary sweep, which re-scanned every cell.\n    for (size_t r = rBegin; r < rEnd; ++r) {\n        const size_t z = r / ny;\n        const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n        const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n        Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n            std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        } else {\n            out[0] = in[0];\n            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // The interior sweep is blocked in x: within one column block the values of\n    // the z-neighbour planes stay in cache until they are reused as centre\n    // values, instead of being evicted by a whole intervening x-y plane.\n    constexpr size_t kBlockX = 192;\n    for (size_t xb = 1; xb + 1 < nx; xb += kBlockX) {\n        const size_t xEnd = std::min(xb + kBlockX, nx - 1);\n        for (size_t r = rBegin; r < rEnd; ++r) {\n            const size_t z = r / ny;\n            const size_t y = r - z * ny;\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) continue;\n\n            const Real* __restrict in = input + r * nx;\n            Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;\n\n            // Same summation order and division as the scalar reference, so\n            // results are bit-for-bit identical.\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = xb; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - plane] + in[x + plane]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma omp barrier\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i])) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering).  Raw aligned allocations are used\n    // instead of std::vector so that the buffers are not pre-faulted by a\n    // single-threaded zero fill; the parallel first touch below distributes the\n    // pages across the NUMA nodes according to the loop partitioning.\n    constexpr size_t kAlign = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(Real) + kAlign - 1) / kAlign) * kAlign;\n    Real* grid1 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    Real* grid2 = static_cast<Real*>(std::aligned_alloc(kAlign, bytes));\n    if (grid1 == nullptr || grid2 == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grids\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    initializeGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);  // first touch of the second buffer\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n            } else {\n                stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalGridVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalGridVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":170,"oldLines":9,"newStart":170,"newLines":6,"lines":["         Real* __restrict out = output + r * nx;","         if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {","             std::memcpy(out, in, nx * sizeof(Real));","-        } else {","-            out[0] = in[0];","-            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];","         }","     }"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
